{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"hops","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"hopping","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"hopped","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"hopped","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To jump a short distance."],"synonyms":[{"word":"jump"},{"word":"leap"}],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To jump on one foot."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Sorry, can't chat.
